   CHANGE LOG v3.5.129 - MEASUREMENT SPOT-ON (the telemetry the dashboard/boards actually run on). Audited the whole capture chain against the new density metric and found four inaccuracies, all fixed: (1) the per-wall board (lockin_walls) posted RAW cube-fill as "density" · it now posts the STRUCTURED density (the same wallSolidity that drives the grade), so board density == grade density. (2) The session summary posted avgWallPct / cubeEff / densityPct as NULL · now computed from the per-wall log (avg wall height %, avg raw cube-fill %, avg structured density %). (3) app_ver was frozen at a stale '2.4.2' (~40 versions old) so EVERY posted session was mislabeled and unsegmentable · now a single LI_APP_VER stamp that bumps with the game. (4) snapWall never captured the structured density · it does now, so the per-wall log, the board, and the session summary all read the same number the grade does. Verified: snapWall carries density, summary computes avgWallPct=50/cubeEff=45/densityPct=85 from a wall log (no nulls), app_ver=3.5.129. Grade, seal payout, per-wall board, and session summary are now one consistent density. NEXT: placement ghost (release blocker), leaderboard pass-2, tire nesting.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.128 - DENSITY/INTEGRITY OVERHAUL (grade credibility · now the boards take real signed-in data). Density was raw fill up to wall height (the v3.5.50 solidity), so two walls with the same integrity graded the same even if one was a loose tower of pillars and floaters. Density is now the BLEND of two axes (Vince's spec): the SHELF axis (row fill inside the bounding box · full shelves beat scattered columns) TIMES the COLUMN axis (each occupied column's fill from the floor up to its own top · punishes floating pieces and buried voids). You can't max one axis to hide the other; a perfect wall (full shelves, all grounded) still = 1.0. This single wallSolidity() feeds both the grade gate (densHistory) AND the seal payout, so the reward is honest too. Verified metric: perfect=1.0, two full shelves=1.0, lone pillar=0.06, floating slab=0.008, five spread columns=0.29, a good wall w/ a few gaps=0.96. Verified grade at equal integrity: elite grounded wall (0.97 dens)=A, good (0.90)=B, loose/floaty (0.42)=D, cheap pillars (0.29)=F · structure now decides the grade, and A is reserved for elite loads. Does NOT touch the WIN/PERFECT-LOAD gate (that uses raw pack% · unchanged). NEXT: placement ghost (release blocker), leaderboard pass-2, tire nesting.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.127 - SIGN-IN PROMPT (a playtester: "add a confirmation when you click a game while not signed in · log in or continue as guest"). Launching any scored game while not clocked in (Trailer-Load, Daily, Practice, Gauntlet, Tour, Smalls Sort) now pops a prompt: CLOCK IN / SIGN UP (opens the account tab) or CONTINUE AS GUEST (plays now, nothing saved). guardLaunch() gates each menu card; picking guest sets _guestAck so it never nags again this session, and if you sign in later isSignedIn() short-circuits the gate entirely. Training (a no-score demo) is not gated. Large legible copy, uniform action cards, no fine print. Verified: signed-out shows the prompt (no launch), CONTINUE AS GUEST runs the pending game + stops nagging, signed-in launches straight through, CLOCK IN opens the account tab. This closes a playtester's entire pen-test list. NEXT: back to Vince.

   CHANGE LOG v3.5.125 - SLIP vs COLLAPSE (Vince's call: "slips are okay, that's how you learn"). collapseFreeStack logged EVERY physics shed as a "collapse" on the record · so a single loose piece read as a collapse (a playtester's "1 collapse on a run I never collapsed"). Now 1-2 pieces coming loose is a SLIP: its own counter (S.slips), a light touch (integrity -5 not -18, small shake, gentle "😅 SLIP · piece came loose, back on the belt" nudge), and it does NOT hit the record or the collapse-escalation ladder. Only 3+ pieces is a real COLLAPSE (full cost + escalation). A dropped HAZMAT is a hurt-somebody event at ANY size and still ends the run. Verified: 1pc + 2pc = slip (0 collapses), 3pc = 1 collapse, hazmat slip still schedules sent-home. (Slips are tracked now so levels/achievements can farm them out later.) NEXT: box labels reflect real box attributes, sign-in prompt.

   CHANGE LOG v3.5.119 - JAILBREAK KILL pt.2: HOLD-R ON THE HOME SCREEN + the _shown() root cause. a playtester: "holding r makes the home-screen chat go crazy; hold r and right-click on home screen, site reloads." The R (rotate) and B (break-jam) key handlers sat ABOVE the kbPlayActive() gate, so they fired on the menu.    CHANGE LOG v3.5.102 - DAILY-SUBMIT BUG FIX (score not saving) + briefing text. THE BUG (Vince: signed in, played daily, still got prompted to "sign in to share your score," signed in again, nothing saved): zSaveSession set uid from j.user.id ONLY · but a token-refresh response omits j.user, so a background refresh wiped ZDATA.session.uid to null while you were still signed in. dailyFinish keys on uid → saw you as anonymous → the anon "sign in to claim" CTA, and zSubmitDaily bailed without posting. FIX: new zJwtSub() reads the uid from the access token's `sub` claim (always present); uid now = j.user.id OR the token sub OR the prior uid · it can never be wiped by a refresh. PLUS: signing in while a just-finished daily sits on the anon CTA now auto-reposts the score (zSaveSession re-runs dailyFinish when the daily-result panel is up). Verified in-browser: a refresh response with no j.user keeps uid + zLoggedIn()=true; fresh token-only session decodes uid from sub. Also: briefing rule 7 no longer reveals "a water break clears it" (a hidden feature) · now "lay it flat before the door opens." Gates clean.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.101 - TIRES REDRAWN (rounded rect + real skins + see-through hole). Vince's exact spec: a tire viewed straight-on 2D is a ROUNDED-CORNER RECTANGLE, not an oval · so the render now CLIPS THE CORNERS of the cell rect (roundRect, radius ~26% of the short side) and fills it with the submitted tire skin (tire1-5.png, seed-picked), instead of the old ellipse-clip that squashed the skin into an oval. The hollow is now a SEE-THROUGH hole (destination-out punch at ~60% so the wall/box/nested layer behind shows through · the same semi-transparent authenticity as the bags), with a hub rim and nested-contents dots inside it. Fully guarded: falls back to a dark fill if a skin isn't loaded, to a plain rect if roundRect is unavailable, try/catch around every draw. Verified NON-BREAKING in-browser: all 5 tire skins load, roundRect supported, the draw frame runs with no thrown error. NEEDS VINCE'S EYEBALL for the actual look · screenshots are wedged this session, so the visual is unconfirmed (the mechanism is his spec exactly; tuning the radius/hole-size is a quick follow-up). Retired the card11 gray-box tire backdrop (the skin now fills the cell).
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.100 - CHUTE-LEAK BUG FIX + SMALLS-CRUSH RULE. (1) THE CHUTE BUG (Smokeh: "any game mode"): BELT_PIECE_COUNT is a mutable global, and level mode's initMechanic()/clearMechanic() slam it to 6 and NEVER restore it · so after Tour (or the menu demo touching a mechanic), a 6-piece chute leaked into every mode after, incl. a fresh Trailer-Load. FIX: new BELT_DEFAULT=10; restartGame() restores it for every non-level start (`if(gameMode!=='level')BELT_PIECE_COUNT=BELT_DEFAULT`); level still sets its own after. Verified: leaked 6 → 10 on Trailer-Load/Practice/Gauntlet/Daily, chute refills to 10. (2) SMALLS-CRUSH: heavy freight (wc>=5) dropped on an UNSUPPORTED smalls bag now crushes it exactly like a fragile · you're smashing the customers' packages inside. checkFragileCrush() targets isFrag OR isBag/small; same protections (locked row / lateral support = safe); routes through the FRAGILE_CRUSH warning + crunch penalty with a smalls-aware callout. Verified: heavy-on-bag/small crushes, heavy-on-regular doesn't, locked/supported bag is safe, light freight is fine. Gates clean.    CHANGE LOG v3.5.98 - NEXT-TRAILER RHYTHM FIX (menu doesn't interrupt). The always-on centered end-popup used to ambush you the instant you hit ~85% · right in the flow.    CHANGE LOG v3.5.97 - PHYSICS SWEEP #5: DIAGONAL / STAIRCASE (sweep COMPLETE, 6/6). Case-A confirmed by the jailbreaker's staircase screenshot. RULE: a box is "overhanging" if any bottom cell hangs over air (less than full support). ONE overhang is legal (you're squeezing a piece in); an overhang stacked ON ANOTHER overhang is the staircase/diagonal lean → BOTH packages fall. EXEMPT: wide flat slabs (>=2.2x wider than tall) overhang safely, so neither a slab load nor a slab support triggers it. New isOverhanging()/isWideSlab()/diagonalViolation(); slots into the post-place chain (heavy → tall-thin → diagonal → freestack). CRITICAL: does NOT touch running-bond · those bricks are FULLY supported (offset seams, nothing over air) so isOverhanging() is false. Verified in-browser: staircase second-hang FAILS, running-bond does NOT fire, single-overhang-on-solid allowed, wide-slab stacked overhangs exempt. THE PHYSICS SWEEP IS DONE · all six mechanics (tire-floor-reset, column-integrity, heavy-freight, tire-rules, diagonals, collapse-win) shipped + verified. Gates clean.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.96 - PHYSICS SWEEP #6: COLLAPSE-WIN FIX. The exploit: placeBox SCHEDULES a collapse (collapseAnimating=true, fires ~220ms later) BEFORE calling checkWin(), so a doomed tower still stood at win-height when the seal fired · reach threshold with a side column, click NEXT TRAILER, and you advanced before it fell ("I shouldn't have won that"). FIX: checkWin() now returns early while collapseAnimating · a seal can't beat a collapse in motion. The wall re-checks on the next placement, on its honest post-collapse height. Verified in-browser: a 100%-height/density wall with a collapse in motion does NOT seal; the instant the collapse resolves, the honest wall seals. Physics sweep now 5 of 6 (done: tire-floor-reset, column-integrity, heavy-freight, tire-rules, collapse-win). REMAINING: #5 DIAGONALS · deferred: there is NO diagonal code, so it's an emergent staircase-lean and a blind fix risks breaking legit running-bond (offset brick-laying is rewarded). Needs the exact exploit shape before build. Gates clean.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.95 - PHYSICS SWEEP #4: TIRE RULES (locked-freight + lift discipline). THREE sub-rules. (4a) A vertical (on-edge) tire rolls and can't bear a load · UNLESS it's LOCKED IN: pinned in a locked shelf row OR braced on BOTH sides (a neighbor or the trailer wall). onVerticalTire() now exempts a locked vertical tire (new tireLockedIn helper) so a pinned tire legitimately carries freight. (4b) SET-IN tires don't budge to a lift: a tire that's part of a stack (a tire directly above/below) OR has any load resting on top now refuses liftBox with a banner · clear what's on top first (new tireSetIn helper). A lone, unloaded tire still lifts fine. (4c) A NESTED tire is STUCK · a tire with anything nested in its hollow can't be lifted ("something's in there"). Verified in-browser: unbraced vertical tire topples its load / braced + locked-row tires bear freight; lifting the top of a 2-tire stack is blocked while a lone tire lifts; a nested tire won't lift. Sweep item #4 of 6 (done: tire-floor-reset, column-integrity, heavy-freight, tire-rules; next: diagonals → collapse-win). Gates clean.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.94 - PHYSICS SWEEP #3: HEAVY FREIGHT DEMANDS A FULL BASE. isLegal() grants up to 25% overhang; heavy freight (wc>=4, non-tire) no longer gets that grace. If ANY of a heavy box's bottom cells sits over air (less than FULL support from below), it auto-collapses · itself AND the box it was crushing · sheds to the belt with a HEAVY FREIGHT, NO FULL BASE banner. New heavyUnsupported(cell); runs FIRST in the post-place priority chain (heavy → tall-thin → freestack). Light freight (wc<4: smalls/bags/fills) keeps the 25% grace; tires exempt (own rules). Verified in-browser: heavy-on-floor holds, heavy-overhang collapses + takes the box under it, light overhang holds, heavy-full-base holds, tire-overhang exempt. Sweep item #3 of 6 (done: tire-floor-reset, column-integrity, heavy-freight-collapse; next: locked-tire/lift rules → diagonals → collapse-win). Gates clean.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.93 - PHYSICS SWEEP #2: COLUMN INTEGRITY (tall-thin verticals). A FREESTANDING tall-thin pillar (≤2 wide, ≥4 tall, not braced on both sides by a neighbor OR the trailer wall) can only bear a vertical load of height floor(0.5·pillarHeight) on top. A 2×5 holds a 2×2 (floor 2.5); a 2×3 up there snaps the column and the load sheds back to the belt with a COLUMN INTEGRITY banner. New tallThinViolation(cell) runs FIRST in the post-place stability check (before worstFreeStack). Braced/walled pillars are exempt (no false positives on real walls). Constrains moonshots to true outliers and chokes the sideways-NC-for-height exploit. Verified in-browser: 2×3-on-2×5 snaps, 2×2-on-2×5 holds, 2×3-on-2×6 holds, 2×4-on-2×6 snaps, 2×5-on-2×4 snaps, braced-pillar exempt, short-pillar exempt. Sweep item #2 of 6 (done: tire-floor-reset, column-integrity; next: heavy-freight-collapse → locked-tire/lift rules → diagonals → collapse-win). Gates clean.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.92 - PHYSICS SWEEP #1: TIRE FLOOR-RESET NERF (kills the tire moonshot). Two surgical changes to worstFreeStack(). (1) A tire acts as a floor · resetting the freestack for whatever rests above · ONLY when genuinely GROUNDED: on the real floor OR a flat locked-in wall row. Tire-on-tire and tire-on-box no longer reset (that chain was the exploit: stack tires to reset the tower forever, then freight rode up "grounded"). (2) Tires are now TRANSPARENT to the freestack · they never count toward the topple length, so a pure tire tower stacks as high as you want and never self-topples, while freight above a non-grounded tire reads as ONE continuous tower (no reset). Verified in-browser on reloaded code: legit 2-tire+box = stable; 5-tire+3-box moonshot = UNSTABLE (freight counted, tires skipped); pure 8-tire tower = stable; tapered pyramid + 4-box control unchanged.    CHANGE LOG v3.5.67 - THE HEY WINDOW (throw is now a real risk/reward mechanic). Throwing a box grabs you a smalls bag FAST · but the manager sees it: "HEY · " opens a 2-SECOND WINDOW. DRINK WATER inside it and he says "…THANK YOU FOR STAYING HYDRATED" (the toss slides, no violation). Miss it and he escalates: "YOU ARE NOT ALLOWED TO THROW PACKAGES!" → a minor THROW safety that ladders to an incident on repeat. Combined with the cooldowns (throw 5s / drink 6s) + the escalating throw & drink score penalties, the meta is: grab a bag when you need one and get away with it · as long as it isn't every box. Removed the old throw-pardons-a-warning model (drinking is the cover now). THROW added to the minor-offense set + safety labels.    CHANGE LOG v3.5.66 - COLLAPSE ESCALATION + throw/drink cooldowns + settings de-slop. (1) COLLAPSE IS A LEARNING CURVE now, not an instant violation: 1st-2nd collapse = a playful "whoopsie" (no record), 3rd = the trainer notices ("your wall keeps collapsing · fix it"), 4th = a safety warning on the record, 5th+ = a violation/incident. The narrative escalates like a real floor: trainer notices → says something → manager comes to watch → writes it up. (Hazmat-in-a-collapse still = round over, checked first.) Both collapse paths (column-topple + integrity-crash) route through collapseEscalation(). (2) THROW + DRINK COOLDOWNS: neither can be spammed · throw 5s, drink 6s, the button greys out while cooling. With the escalating score penalties, the escape-valve meta is now strategic (and optional). (3) SETTINGS descriptions de-slopped: dropped the "full recorded set lands later" / "more palettes can follow" unfulfilled-promise lines and the clunky "(fully off when OFF)".    CHANGE LOG v3.5.65 - VERTICAL-TIRE PHYSICS + hazmat-collapse + throw penalty + level-name cleanup. (1) VERTICAL TIRE = unstable base: a rotated (on-edge) tire can't bear a load, so ANY piece placed one higher TOPPLES (onVerticalTire). (2) VERTICAL TIRE-STACKING ladder: stacking a tire on top of another tire escalates · 1st warning, 2nd incident, 3rd practice trailer, 4th round OVER ("your wall came down · you got hurt"). (3) HAZMAT in ANY collapse = round over ("you're going to hurt somebody") · a dropped hazmat is a hurt-somebody event, no matter the stack. (4) THROW PENALTY (perfect symmetry with the drink rule): 4th throw -100, 5th -200, 6th -300... per wall · kills the "throw forever → 1000 smalls from bags" exploit (you CAN, it just goes deep negative). (5) COMMENTARY: the abusive "use your brain" smalls line → "Fill the gaps." (6) LEVEL NAMES: L1 hub was a duplicate of its city (both "Columbus, OH · CMH") → "John Glenn Columbus Intl"; L5 was "THE INCHEON OVERNIGHT" (dupe of L7 Chicago) set in "Seoul" → "THE INCHEON RUSH" / "Incheon, South Korea · ICN".    CHANGE LOG v3.5.61 - THE "LOCKED-SHELF MOONSHOT" + throw reachability + post-sort quality slop. (1) COLUMN: the jailbreaker's "if the bottom is supported we can still break your game" was real - worstFreeStack anchored on isRowLockedIn(y), which is true for EVERY row above any locked shelf, so completing one shelf made the freestanding scan break at the very top and you could tower infinitely above it. Now it breaks only AT the actual locked-shelf row (G.tetrisRows[y]); the shelf still anchors what rests on it, but a freestanding tower built on top is evaluated and topples normally. (2) THROW/WATER buttons pulled IN from the far gutters to flank the play field (were unreachable / choppy at the screen edge; throw "didn't work" because the cursor couldn't get to it). No grade-math change.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.60 - COLUMN RULE v5 (dimensional caps + moonshot kill). Two additions to worstFreeStack, both tunable. (1) MOONSHOT FIX: being "sandwiched" (boxed on both sides) now only ANCHORS a stack if at least one side is a real GROUND-CONNECTED pillar (solidBelow: filled continuously down to the floor or a locked shelf) or the trailer wall - two floating towers bracing each other no longer hold each other up above height 4. A genuine solid wall still stands (its neighbors reach the ground); a gap-braced tower now topples. (2) DIMENSIONAL CAP: a freestanding stack has a max CELL height keyed to its narrowest footprint - 1-wide 6, 2-wide 9, 3-wide 7, 4-wide 6, 5-wide+ 5 (strict taper +2). So a wide flat slab tips sooner (his "5x2 shouldn't go 3-high" = 6 cells > cap 5) while a tall-narrow box is allowed higher (his "2x6 up to ~9"). Existing rules kept: freestanding 4+, non-taper 3+, smalls-in-3+ all still collapse. Knobs live in freestandCellCap()/FREESTAND_TAPER_BONUS. No metric-capture change.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.59 - SAFETY SYSTEM + THROW/WATER side buttons. Two tracked categories now: WARNINGS (recoverable) and INCIDENTS (on the record). Ladder: a warning you DON'T mitigate, repeated as the SAME offense, escalates to an INCIDENT; 5 incidents = sent to a fresh beginner PRACTICE trailer (clean slate, record still stands - NOT game over). Mitigation: lock a shelf (instant neutralize), drink WATER (clears one open minor warning), or THROW the piece (pardons a minor only). HAZMAT-high and COLLAPSES are incidents outright - no pardon (a collapse is now a documented safety incident, so 2 = the talking-to; removed the old separate collapse grade term to avoid double-counting). HYDRATION is now a real safety: 75% up the wall with no water = warning, top it bone-dry = incident; OVER-drinking to farm warning-washes = SABOTAGE (logged). Washed safeties are STILL published (the "1501 safeties, 1490 for throwing boxes" bit - report now shows warnings total, washed, thrown-away, sabotage flags, sent-to-training). NEW side buttons: WATER (left gutter) + THROW (right gutter), emoji placeholders ready for Vince's icons; THROW always spawns a bag of smalls. Live SAFE meter in the control row.    CHANGE LOG v3.5.57 - GRADE HONESTY (the "A on a broken wall" bug). The POST-SORT REPORT hero grade was STILL running the old v2.1.39 lenient formula (no density gate, A at 85, collapse only -10) while the strict density-gated sessionGrade() only fed the live OPERATIONS drawer. That two-formula split is how a 48%-dense, 78-integrity, 6-collapse wall read as an A. The report now runs the SAME strict sessionGrade() as ops. Also fixed the stale SESSION integrity (100% while the wall was 78%): sealWall captured density but never integrity, so a sealed wall's real number never entered the session average and it defaulted toward the seed 100. NEW finalizeWall() is now the single source of truth - captures EXACTLY one integrity+density sample per wall (idempotent via G._finalized) the moment it's sealed / clocked-out / failed; every other push site now routes through it. And relabeled the report's "Wall fill %" to "Wall height %" - it measured ceiling-reach, not fill (one column to the roof read 100%); "Cube fill %" stays the true density.    CHANGE LOG v3.5.56 - FLOATER SWEEP after collapse. Core column meta is solved (can't 1xN/NxN to the moon in normal play); remaining "floaties" are collapse artifacts -- when a stack sheds, boxes that rested on it were left hanging (no gravity to re-settle). settleFloaters() now sweeps after every column collapse: any box with nothing under it (floor or another box, via boxHasSupport) sheds back to the belt too, recursing until the wall is clean.    CHANGE LOG v3.5.55 - COLUMN RULE v4 (the adjacency bug). Screenshots: parallel columns + combs never collapsed. ROOT CAUSE: the support check counted ANY single side-neighbor as support, so two pillars "held each other up." FIX: a cell is only anchored if SANDWICHED (a box on BOTH sides, trailer wall counts) OR under a LOCKED SHELF OR a tire seated on floor/another-tire. A single neighbor is NOT support -> gappy/crooked/edge/parallel pillars now fall. Added: a smalls/bag anywhere in a 3+ freestanding stack collapses; a tire on a BOX (not floor/tire) is part of the run. Rule: any freestanding 4+ (even tapered, the n-1 rule), non-taper 3+, or smalls-in-3+ -> collapse. SAFETY schema locked (build next): 2 warnings then escalate, the 2nd UNMITIGATED same offense = an incident, ~5 -> beginner "practice" trailer (not game-over); water clears ONE warning (over-drinking = sabotage); throw = pardon for a MINOR safety only, never hazmat; collapse = documented incident (2 = the talking-to); hazmat-high IS hazmat-mid-wall.    CHANGE LOG v3.5.54 - COLUMN RULE v3 (taper-aware) + collapse RESOLVES + demo popup. Friend still stacked smalls to the ceiling off a wide 5x6/4x4 base, and the scan-all was re-firing the collapse EVERY placement. Rewrote: worstFreeStack() reads the DISTINCT boxes in each column's freestanding run (no lateral neighbor; a locked shelf/tire ends it) and flags NON-tapering stacks (any box >= the width of the one below = same-width/top-heavy, e.g. 1x1-on-1x1 smalls) vs a strict taper (4x4/3x3/2x2/1x1, exempt).    CHANGE LOG v3.5.53 - COLUMN RULE FIX (playtest jailbreaks). v3.5.52 only checked the placed box's columns and treated one lateral neighbor as full support, so a friend could: stack smalls forever on a wide box, build a separate column while an old 5-high one stood, and pillars persisted.    CHANGE LOG v3.5.45 - CLIPBOARD-BLANK FIX (cross-device briefing bug). The pre-sort clipboard showed with NO text on some machines / intermittently on phones. ROOT CAUSE: the HUB-voice briefing (#brief-hub) was retired from the HTML, but briefTab() still ran at boot · when the saved voice (localStorage liVoice) was 'hub', it set #brief-std to display:none and tried to show the missing #brief-hub, leaving the clipboard blank. Fingerprint matched exactly: fine at voice=std (work computer), blank at voice=hub (personal computer), intermittent by device. FIX: briefTab now only swaps to brief-hub if it actually exists; otherwise it ALWAYS shows brief-std regardless of voice. No scoring change.
   CHANGE LOG v3.5.44 - PICK BY ZONE (mobile release blocker · "nothing happened" / off-piece grabs). Selecting freight required tapping INSIDE a piece's exact rect; tiny pieces were near-impossible to hit and sometimes needed tapping off-piece. Regular box palettes are hsl() strings, not hex - and since the conveyability gate now routes regular freight into NC slots, shx() ran parseInt on 'hsl(...)' and produced '#NaNNaN44', which addColorStop rejects. That threw every frame, aborting drawScene mid-paint = the floating half-rendered screen. shx() is now bulletproof: hex shades exactly as before; hsl/rgba/named/garbage return unchanged (valid CSS, no shade, no crash). Fixes all shx callers, not just the floor slot. No other behavior touched.\n   CHANGE LOG v2.10.9 - LABEL OVERHAUL + THIS-SIDE-UP MECHANIC.    CHANGE LOG v2.10.5 - THE BELT, FIXED AT THE ROOT. Root cause found: the belt is filled by genBeltPiece, NOT genPiece - so every conveyability gate AND the realistic weighting I added (v2.10.0-2.10.4) lived in a function that never touched the belt. That is why 7x2/8x4 kept coming and the mix stayed wrong. NOW: (1) genBeltPiece picks via pickWeighted (realistic freight frequency actually applies); (2) genBeltPiece gates conveyability - non-conveyable freight routes to NC, panic/gauntlet reroll conveyable. A 7x2 or 8x4 cannot reach the belt anymore (non-daily). (3) CLICKABILITY: belt boxes were overlapping perspective sprites, so clicks hit the wrong one. Rebuilt as evenly-spaced NON-overlapping slots (height capped to the slot gap), no bob, and the hit-test now picks the nearest box. Every piece is its own clean target with its W×H shown.\n   CHANGE LOG v2.10.4 - REALISTIC FREIGHT FREQUENCY. Spawns were uniform - a 5x1 was as likely as a 2x2 - so you saw too many long-thin pieces and too few small squares. Now weighted by realism: square/near-square and small-to-standard boxes are common (1x1, 2x1, 2x2, 2x3, 3x3, and the 3x4 Target/Chewy standard), while long-thin (4x1, 5x1, 6x1) are rare but present. Weight = aspect band x area band; knobs are in pieceWeight() for tuning. Applies to small/regular/large belt spawns (and gauntlet). Daily untouched (own deterministic generator).

Live VOIDS stays cube-by-cube.
   CHANGE LOG v2.10.2 - SCORE-FARM EXPLOIT KILLED. Lifting a placed piece and re-placing it re-awarded every bonus (VOID FILLER, CORNER/ANCHOR/CORNERSTONE, and the DOUBLE LOCK +shelf bonus), so a nested tire could be cycled for infinite cubes + pieces-per-wall. Two guards: (1) a piece earns its keystone/void-filler bonus ONCE ever (box._bonusEarned) - re-placement pays nothing and no longer double-counts the piece; (2) a row pays its shelf-lock bonus ONCE per trailer (G.tetrisPaid) - unsealing via lift then re-locking no longer re-pays. tetrisPaid resets with tetrisRows on every trailer. Bulkhead was already guarded (persistent bulkheadRows). Legit first-time scoring unchanged.
   CHANGE LOG v2.10.1 - GRAB OFF THE BELT (the middle belt is now the preselector). The freight boxes riding up the aisle are now CLICKABLE - tap one to pick it up (selected box gets a teal highlight + the wall ghost), then click the wall to place, exactly like the bottom strip but on the real belt. SmallSort-style. drawBeltFreight stores each box's screen rect every frame; the scene pointerup tests those first and sets selBeltIdx. The bottom strip still works as a backstop for THIS build only - confirm the belt grabs clean and the strip gets deleted next (one line).
   CHANGE LOG v2.10.0 - CONVEYABILITY RULE (the belt foundation). Long-thin and bulky freight physically lodges in a conveyor and jams the line, so it is non-conveyable by nature and now routes to NC staging (hand-loaded) instead of riding the belt. Rule: max>=8 OR (max>=7 AND min<=2) => NC. This pulls OVR-LONG 8x3, OVR-WIDE 10x3, PALLET-M 7x2, PALLET-L 9x2, BUNDLE 8x4, FLATSKID 12x2 off the belt and into NC. 6x1 stays conveyable (the limit); 6x6 and 7x3+ convey. Gauntlet (no NC) rerolls to a conveyable piece. Daily is UNTOUCHED (own deterministic generator - reconcile separately with a determinism reset so existing leaderboard scores hold). Belt now only carries conveyable freight - the foundation for grab-off-belt + jam.
   CHANGE LOG v2.9.9 - REAL FREIGHT ON THE BELT (+ reverted the v2.9.8 square-cell shrink). (1) The v2.9.8 17:20 aspect-lock letterboxed/shrank the playfield - reverted to filling the photo wall (BACKDROP_WALL knobs unchanged; re-measure them for the new steel backdrop). (2) drawBeltFreight: the ACTUAL G.belt queue now rides UP the centre aisle to the wall and POOLS there, SmallSort-style - reskinned cardboard, perspective-scaled, front-of-queue pooled at the wall base, ground shadows + belt bob. Reflects real upcoming pieces, render-only (no state, no selection, no determinism touched). This is the freight-up-the-belt mechanic, built - not decoration.
   CHANGE LOG v2.9.8 - SQUARE CELLS + WALL RESKIN. (1) The photo-backdrop wall stretched the 17x20 grid to the image rectangle, so cube cells were non-square and a 5-cube piece could read as 6. The box-stacking grid is now locked to the largest 17:20 rectangle that fits inside the photo wall, centered - cell width === cell height, always, so piece sizes are eyeball-countable like on the dock. Aspect-lock beats filling the photo. (2) Placed boxes on the WALL now take the same card1..cardN cardboard surface as the belt pieces (drawWallBoxes/renderWallBox was a separate render that never got the texture). Seed-stable per piece, cached per-context. Graceful fallback to the kraft palette.
   CHANGE LOG v2.9.7 - RENDER-ON-LOAD FIX (the 'renders weird until I toggle settings' bug). resize() ran ONCE at boot, before fonts loaded and before the backdrop decoded - so scene-wrap reported transitional dimensions and computeGeom() ran with BACKDROP_OK false, leaving the canvas mis-sized and the box-grid locked to the procedural perspective instead of the photo wall; a manual zoom toggle fired a resize event that corrected it. Now the canvas re-fits when layout-shifting things actually arrive: backdrop image load (re-aligns to the photo), document.fonts.ready, window load, plus a deferred tick and an rAF. resize() only touches geometry, never game state.
   CHANGE LOG v2.9.6 - MULTIPLE CARDBOARD SURFACES. The single cardboard.png hook is now an array: drop card1.png through card8.png (tileable kraft swatches) in the repo and each box deterministically picks one by its seed - so a trailer shows varied box surfaces, not one repeated skin. A box always wears the same surface (seed-stable). Patterns are cached per-context and per-surface. Graceful fallback to the kraft palette for any slot with no image. Load as few or as many as you have (1-8); missing numbers just skip.
   CHANGE LOG v2.9.5 - CARDBOARD BOXES. The box palette was vivid (hue 20-60 at 45-65% saturation) so freight read as candy colors, not cardboard. Muted to kraft tones (hue ~27-39, 17-30% saturation, varied lightness) so every standard box reads as corrugated kraft; the existing tape, corrugation, sheen and edge shading now actually look like a shipping box, and the colored type LABELS (FRAGILE/GLASS/PRI/hazmat) still carry readability. Added a center flap seam line. IMAGE PATH: drop a tileable cardboard.png (kraft texture) in the repo and it pattern-fills every box for a real photographic surface at any size - graceful fallback to the kraft palette if absent.    CHANGE LOG v2.6.9 - MOBILE: TAP AIMS, DROP PLACES (no more blind drops). Root cause of brutal mobile misplacements found: the wall pointer-up handler placed the piece wherever the finger LIFTED - under your own thumb, sight unseen, on release. On phones now, tapping the wall with a piece loaded MOVES the ghost to that cell (clamped to fit) instead of dropping; placement happens only on DROP. So you tap-to-aim, fine-tune with the joystick, and commit deliberately - the piece never drops where you can't see it. Desktop mouse placement unchanged. No scoring change, but mobile incidents should fall sharply now that you can see before you commit.

   CHANGE LOG v2.6.4 · CRISP RENDER (mobile legibility). Root cause of the tiny/blurry mobile text: the canvas backing store was sized in CSS pixels, so on a 2-3x retina phone the browser upscaled a low-res canvas and everything · text included · went soft. Now both canvases render at devicePixelRatio (capped at 3x for perf): backing store = CSS size x DPR, CSS display size unchanged, context scaled by DPR so all drawing and hit-testing still use CSS-pixel coordinates. getPC (pointer->canvas mapping) divides DPR back out so taps/clicks land exactly as before · verified no other code reads the backing-store size, and there's no getImageData/drawImage to disturb. Text and edges are now sharp on phones and retina desktops; non-retina desktop is pixel-identical. No gameplay, scoring, layout, or backend change.

That change · plus mobile pausing a volume-0 element · is why the slider felt dead; additionally, raising the music slider now actively starts/resumes playback instead of only setting volume, so it always responds. (Default-off for a public launch stays available as a deliberate later choice, not a silent break.) TOUCH CONTROLS: tap-and-slide was unplayable (fat-finger misses, the ghost vanished at edges, misplacement penalties). Added an on-screen control bar on phones · left/up/down/right nudge the held piece, rotate, and DROP to place · wired to the proven keyboard engine (kbMove/kbPlace), so the ghost clamps in-bounds (never disappears), nudging is exact, and DROP only commits where legal. Tap-to-place still works as a secondary. Desktop unchanged. KNOWN/NEXT: in-canvas text is small on phones (a render-scale pass is needed); magnetic snap still queued. No scoring or backend change.

   CHANGE LOG v2.5.5 · RECOVERY + PERF.    CHANGE LOG v2.5.2 · CSP META (auth unblock). trailer-load.com is GitHub Pages proxied through Cloudflare, so a _headers file does nothing and there is no edge CSP to fight (confirmed: no content-security-policy response header).    CHANGE LOG v2.4.10 · WATCHDOG CAP + RECOVERY SCREEN. Root cause of the console spam: the watchdog re-fired EVERY tick when the heartbeat was frozen (the classic backgrounded-tab signature · lastLoopT=0, rAF suspended, setInterval still ticking), logging thousands of lines and thrashing the loop. Now: a stall triggers at most 3 SILENT auto-revive attempts (one quiet console note); if the loop still can't advance it STOPS retrying and shows a proper RECOVERY SCREEN · a plain-English explanation + RESUME / CHOOSE MODE / RELOAD. The raw amber diagnostic strip is retired (the recovery screen carries the state string for screenshots). The v2.4.8 visibility gate already prevents this from firing on a backgrounded tab at all; this makes ANY genuine terminal/stuck condition explain itself and offer a way out instead of a silent freeze. No gameplay change.
   CHANGE LOG v2.4.9 · EMPTY-WALL WEDGE: DIAGNOSE + LOOSE END. (1) nextTrailer now clears a pending collapseTimeout · it cleared the NC-hazard and bag-dwell timers but not the collapse timer; a leftover could fire on the next wall (mostly harmless since integrity resets to 100, but now closed per standard). (2) Live state probe: the watchdog publishes window.__LI_STATE every tick with every input/progress flag. When the board ever feels stuck (input dead, a reload fixes it), type __LI_STATE in the console and it names the exact blocking flag so the fix is one line, not a guess. NOTE: console noise like ObjectMultiplex / app-init-liveness / contentscript.js is a browser extension (MetaMask), not the game.
   CHANGE LOG v2.4.8 · WATCHDOG FALSE-ALARM FIX. The render loop runs on requestAnimationFrame, which the browser PAUSES whenever the tab is backgrounded (alt-tab, print-screen, switching windows) · normal behavior. The watchdog runs on setInterval, which keeps ticking in the background, so it saw the paused heartbeat and wrongly fired the amber 'loop stalled · auto-restarted' strip on return. The watchdog now skips while document.hidden is true and re-baselines the heartbeat on tab-return, so backgrounding no longer triggers a false stall (and can no longer double-schedule the loop). Genuine stalls (frozen heartbeat while the tab is visible and the game should be running) are still caught and healed. No gameplay change.
   CHANGE LOG v2.4.7 · POST-SORT REPORT now reachable per wall: a ◆ POST-SORT REPORT button added to the wall-seal popup (previously only on the session-end screen, so in shift play you never reached it); it opens the same readout for the just-sealed wall. Defensive: the refillBelt while-loop is now hard-capped (240 iterations) so no degenerate belt state can silent-freeze the game. Traced the end-of-wall + telemetry path top-to-bottom · fillTelemetryTiles, the union-find, and the belt loop are all light and non-looping, so the heavy-collection stall theory did not match the code.
   CHANGE LOG v2.4.5 · POST-SORT REPORT (the readout, in-game). A 'POST-SORT REPORT' button on the end screen opens a dense clipboard sheet: every metric at WALL / SESSION / LIFETIME, six categorized blocks (throughput, load quality, safety+docs, mechanics) + a verdict band, matching the approved mock. SESSION + WALL are live (per-wall snapshots captured at each seal feed session averages; the four sub-scores are read from the end-screen tiles already computed). LIFETIME is fetched from the lockin_lifetime view when signed in, and reads 'LOG IN TO TRACK' (dashes) when anon. Printable (browser print = a clean paper scorecard). Build-once component · this is the same readout the profile/instructor views will reuse. Slot-occupancy + pack-fill utility still pending (need per-frame plumbing); shown as ' · ' for now.
   CHANGE LOG v2.4.4 · REAL FREEZE FIX + ON-SCREEN DIAGNOSTICS. The mid-wall freeze was NOT the render loop (v2.4.0 mis-targeted it) · it was endGame: it cancels the animation frame FIRST, then runs a long unguarded chain (getElementById().textContent + fillTelemetryTiles() + zEndSession()), revealing the end overlay only on the LAST line. Any throw there = loop already dead + end screen never shown = hard freeze with the lose sound already played. FIX: both end paths (endGameShift + generic endGame) wrap fill/telemetry in try/catch and ALWAYS reveal the end overlay even if telemetry throws · never stranded on a dead frame; worst case is a partial end screen showing its REASON (so a wrongly-firing terminal is self-identifying). DIAGNOSTICS: liShowErr() paints a dismissable red strip with the error message + stack the first time anything throws (loop OR endGame) so the next incident is screenshot-and-fixed, not guessed. Pause stays live-play only; the always-on end screen is the terminal exit.
   CHANGE LOG v2.4.3 · COUNTERS + SFX CUES. Wired the metrics that were writing null/0, and gave the new events SOUND (audio-learner support). NEW SFX: 'stage' (low rubber thunk · fires when a SET of 2+ SP pieces drops into staging, so a tire batch is an AUDIBLE heads-up, not a silent ambush) and 'dwell' (descending 3-beep warning, distinct from the hazard klaxon) on both SP and smalls dwell. NEW/REAL COUNTERS: ncDwellWarn, smallsDwellWarn, stagingActions, ncSpawned/ncPlaced + smallsSpawned (reusing bagsPlaced) -> placed-over-spawned UTILITY % is now real for SP and smalls; lift-lock denials now fold into rejectedDrops (your spec: denied actions count, misclicks don't). S init carries all new fields across every reset. avgWallPct/cubeEff/densityPct stay null til per-wall capture lands with the sheet. No gameplay/balance change · capture + audio only.
   CHANGE LOG v2.4.2 · DATA SPINE: canonical summary contract + user_id. The session payload now writes the exact keys the lockin_lifetime view reads (camelCase: peakPph, avgPph, avgIntegrity, shelvesLocked, doubleLocks, nests, incTotal + per-type hazHigh/hazCrush/seg/killer, crunches, collapses, recoveries, hydrationBreaks, rotations, lifts, rejectedDrops, bagsBroken/Placed, hazmatLocks, bonuses). Incident-by-type and bonuses are DERIVED from data already tracked (no fragile event-site edits). Flat columns (total_score/best_pph/walls_loaded/avg_integrity/incident_count) are now populated too, so simple dashboard queries work without digging into jsonb. user_id is stamped from the auth session so a logged-in player's rows roll up into their lifetime. app_ver no longer hardcoded to 2.1.38. Not-yet-tracked metrics (dwell warnings, staging, utility %, per-wall fill/cube/density) write null/0 and get their counters next ship. Capture-only; no gameplay change.
   CHANGE LOG v2.4.1 · TUNING: farm-tire spawn set 6 -> 4. A TIRE-FARM spawn was staging 6 SP slots at once, which could jam staging from 6/8 and tip a strong run (642 PPH, 9 shelves, 8 doubles) into an unavoidable NC-staging hazard with no room to clear. Farm sets now stage 4 (DB=2, STD/MC=4 unchanged), leaving headroom to work the wall down. The hazard is still a real fail condition · just survivable. Nest capacity (a farm tire's hollow still holds 6 smalls) is unchanged; that's physical.
   CHANGE LOG v2.4.0 · FREEZE FIX (hard bug). The game could hard-freeze mid-wall (animation suspended, clicks still firing) · classic dead requestAnimationFrame loop: the frame reschedule was the LAST line of loop(), so ANY throw in a frame killed the loop permanently. Root throw found: the tire render fed ellipse() a radius from Math.max(3,_trx*0.92), but Math.max(3,NaN)===NaN, and ellipse() throws IndexSizeError on a non-finite radius · fatal once tires were on the wall. FIX (two layers): (1) tire radii/centre are now finite-guarded before any ellipse()/arc(). (2) the whole loop body is wrapped so it can NEVER die · a bad frame is caught, logged once to console + window.__LI_ERR {msg,stack,mode,placed}, and the next frame is always scheduled. A single bad frame is now a self-healing skip, not a freeze. Freeze-fix ONLY this ship; readout wiring is next.
   CHANGE LOG v2.3.3 · POLISH: COLORBLIND GRADE RING. The end-screen letter grade used a JS-hardcoded green->red hue ramp · the one grade color that slipped past the [data-cb] CSS remap (the four sub-score bars already remap via --ok/--warn/--danger). When Colorblind Mode is on, the grade hue now swaps to a CVD-safe teal->amber diverging ramp (no green-vs-red reliance), on-palette. The letter itself always carried the grade; this fixes the color channel for red-green CVD too. Procurement-relevant for school buyers (508/ADA). No other change.

   CHANGE LOG v2.3.0 · FINISHING PASS pt.1: LIVE BELT.    CHANGE LOG v2.2.2 · STOP COLLAPSING GOOD WALLS. The v2.1.38 _maxExp rule force-collapsed any column sitting 5+ above its neighbour · but that is the NORMAL uneven top profile of a real, supported wall, not a free-standing pole. It was dropping B-grade, fully-supported, locked-in walls into the collapse zone, and the piece the collapse yanked left the phantom gap you saw. REMOVED the _maxExp force-collapse. It still DOCKS the integrity score (a spiky profile reads lower), it just never forces a collapse anymore. Collapse now requires a GENUINE free-standing pole (both neighbour columns empty, tall) or an EXPOSED fragile crushed by a heavy. A bridged big box over a gap, a tall supported section, an uneven top · all safe.
   CHANGE LOG v2.2.1 · LOCKED IN IS PROTECTED (crush/collapse fix). v2.1.38's heavy-on-fragile COLLAPSE scan ran across the WHOLE grid and ignored locked rows, lateral support, and already-crushed state · so a fragile that locked into a shelf and then got legitimately built on top of would trigger a wall collapse, anywhere on the wall, nowhere near where you placed. That's backwards: locking in is the SAFE move. FIX: the collapse now fires ONLY for a genuinely exposed fragile · not in a locked row, no lateral support, not already crushed · with a HEAVY piece (wc>=4, up from 3) directly on it. Locked-in pieces and laterally-supported pieces are now immune to the fragile-collapse, matching the crush mechanic which already protected them. You can't be punished for a shelf you locked in clean.

FIX: boot now clears any inline display and shows the briefing via its CSS class only; acknowledgeBriefing hides it with an inline display:none that can't be overridden. Shows reliably on first load, closes reliably on 'OPEN THE DOOR'.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.37 · WATER / HYDRATION GATE. Water isn't freight · it's YOU. New WATER button + fill meter under the left OPS panel. Rule: you must drink to seal a wall. 0 drinks at seal = you passed out · GAME OVER in shift mode, hard-blocked (can't seal til you drink) in forgiving modes. 1 drink = par. 2 drinks = a flat hydration bonus (+300 CUBES, the everybody-bonus). 3+ = no extra. After ~2 shelves the button goes red and reads DRINK! if you're still dry. Drinks reset every wall. New 'drink' SFX. S.drinks tracked. Simple acknowledgement mechanic · take your water breaks.
   CHANGE LOG v2.1.36 · TIRES PASS 2: NESTING (the hidden veteran move). Select a SMALL (belt or SMALLS bag) and click a placed tire · instead of landing on the wall it seats INTO the tire's hollow, reclaiming dead air. UNTAUGHT on purpose: it's nowhere in the tutorial, and the first time you do it the game fires 'THAT'S NOT IN THE TRAINING · YOU LEARNED THAT.' Capacity by tire (cube-cost): DB/MC=2, STD=3, FARM=6 · stuff what fits, hollow-full denies. REWARD MODEL: nested cube is BONUS CUBES (cube efficiency climbs past 100%) while geometric DENSITY · the true airspace measure · is unchanged. That's the dual metric the dashboard wants: CUBES = cube efficiency (can exceed 100), DENSITY = airspace (<=100). HAZMAT SHIELD: a small hazmat nested in the hollow is off the grid entirely · it can't trigger a hazmat-high or segregation violation, it's leak-shielded, and it pays a bonus. Nested pieces ride WITH the tire if you lift it. New 'nest' SFX. S.nests tracked. DECISION: nesting is after-placement (drop into the visible hole) · visible + teachable-by-discovery.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.34 · NO MORE FLOATING SLOP + 100% SFX. (1) A load-bearing piece (anything stacked on top of it) CANNOT be lifted · try it and you get a deny buzz + 'it's holding a load' coach line. No more pulling the bottom box and leaving the stack floating on thin air. (2) Lifting a piece OUT of a locked shelf unseals it: -150 cubes + the lock is lost (don't unbuild what works, unless you're chasing a finishing/hazmat play).    CHANGE LOG v2.1.33 · TOPPLE THE POLE + easy mechanics wins. (1) Lateral support: a column rising above BOTH neighbours has nothing holding it sideways · that overhang is now penalised hard, and a TALL FREE-STANDING POLE (both neighbour columns empty, >=5 high) drops integrity into the collapse zone so it actually topples instead of standing there at 100%. (2) Collapse now sheds the top of a free-standing pole regardless of weight, so light poles visibly fall (before, collapse only grabbed heavy pieces and a light tower never moved). (3) Illegal placement now gives feedback · a reject buzz, a small shake, and a rate-limited 'NO SUPPORT THERE' coach line · instead of failing silently. (4) Sealing a wall plays a win fanfare.    CHANGE LOG v2.1.32 · STRUCTURAL HONESTY (no collapse re-armed). The integrity SCORE was lenient: the per-piece penalty only fired for heavy pieces (wc>=4), so a light narrow tower scored ~100 even though it's structurally absurd. Added geometry penalties that tell the truth without collapsing the wall: (1) column-height SPIKE penalty · a tall column standing well above its neighbours' average is an unsupported tower and is docked; (2) NARROW FOOTPRINT penalty · a tall wall spread across too few columns is docked; (3) open VOIDS now cost integrity (capped); (4) COG/balance penalty tightened · it now bites from 15% off-centre instead of only past 30%. Balance meter readout also tightened (STABLE <7%, CAUTION <16%). Locked shelves still protect the wall (lock floor unchanged) and collapse stays soft. Knobs are labelled for tuning.

Regular/training/level now FORGIVING · those pressures warn+coach but never end the round, so beginners can learn. Building physics (collapse) unchanged everywhere. Work-stoppage already level-gated.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.9 · Stats expansion + PPH fix: Best PPH now a tracked PEAK (updated each placement; fixes the 0 bug), Avg PPH (session) split out separately, Best Trailer Avg, Gaps Left, Session Time, Rejected Drops added. Readout now 19 tiles. Richer pool for data-viz.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.8 · Clock-out + full readout: CLOCK OUT (pause menu) ends the round, KEEPS score, shows stats. Readout 5->14 tiles (pieces, shelves, double locks, recoveries, crunches, collapses, rotations, lifts, bags). Session shelf/lock/recovery totals accumulate in S. Layout/font readability = cosmetic sweep.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.7 · Telemetry capture layer: session counters for collapses, rotations, lifts/replacements, bags broken, bags placed, total pieces (added to S). Foundation for the clock-out readout (v2.1.8) and backend capture. No display change yet.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.6 · Hazmat incident tracking: permanent EVENT-based session record (S.incidents) for HAZMAT_HIGH, HAZMAT_CRUSH, SEGREGATION, KILLER. Does NOT un-log on fix (unlike the score, which restores). 2+ = FLAGGED banner + end-screen 'Haz Incidents' stat. The hire/don't-hire metric.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.5 · Hazmat segregation (49 CFR, simplified): different classes need >=2 clear cells; killer pairs (5.1+flammable/gas/corrosive, 1+anything, 8+gas/toxic) hard-violate; same-class 3+ touching chains violate. Live scan folds into the halving multiplier (separate them to restore). One-time breach banner. WALL only; staging-lane segregation = visual pass.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.4 · Column-seam penalty: countColumnSeams() flags >=3 same-footprint pieces stacked with aligned edges (running-bond violation), docked 100/violation live via calcScore (restagger to clear). One-time bricklaying tip. Distinct from getColumnRisk (spawn modulation only).

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.3 · Preselection cost: front belt piece is free; reaching deeper (sourceIdx>0) accumulates G.preselectDepth, docked at 25/step via calcScore. One-time tip on first deep reach. The efficient-loader vs cherry-picker metric axis.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.2 · Staging: staged NC/bag pieces now CARRY to the next trailer in standard mode (initTruck no longer wipes them); a piece you couldn't load rides over. Shift mode already persisted.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.1 · Feedback/UX: lock celebration collapsed to ONE readable pill (off build zone, +pts folded in, 2400/3000ms), de-doubled (5 fires->1), BULKHEAD renamed DOUBLE LOCK, NEXT TRAILER pinned to right side.

   CHANGE LOG v2.1.0 · Pool/taxonomy: tires->NC (real sizes), +10 pro & +11 hazmat pieces, hazard classes 1-9, weighted pickNC (30/25/30/15) + <=17% hazmat governor, hazmat-on-belt drift FIXED (now NC-only), FSPM nc+smalls bump.

   CHANGE LOG v2.0.9 · HAZMAT HALVE-SCORE + QUIETER CELEBRATIONS
   - Hazmat misplacement now HALVES the score (was flat -300). Real-world stakes:
     a hazmat loaded high is an instant write-up. Each currently-misplaced hazmat
     halves again (live grid scan, NOT a one-time counter) · so LIFTING it back
     below waist RESTORES the score. Correcting the mistake un-penalizes you.
   - Celebration banners ('ok' class) capped at 1200ms so the "WOW" clears fast
     and stops blocking the placement zone mid-rhythm. Warnings/danger unchanged.
   - countHazmatViolations(grid) added; scoring uses live state not event tally.

   CHANGE LOG v2.0.8 · HAZMAT ENFORCEMENT (credibility pass)
   - Hazmat placards (HAZMAT/FLAMMABLE/TOXIC) were cosmetic-only; now ENFORCED.
   - New rule: dangerous goods must load below waist height (bottom 50% of wall).
     Real industry/OSHA + 49 CFR practice · first thing a floor vet checks.
   - isHaz flag added to hazmat labels; waist-line violation check on placement.
   - No new art, no spawn change · placards already spawned, just unenforced.
